     1	import type { ZodTypeAny, infer as ZodInfer } from 'zod';
     2	import type { ComponentType } from 'react';
     3	
     4	export type BlockKind = 'prose' | 'component';
     5	
     6	/**
     7	 * BlockCore：headless 层 —— 无 React 依赖，纯逻辑（props schema + MDX 序列化在
     8	 * mdx-bridge 处理）。Core 可被 Adopter 复用并配自定义 UI。
     9	 */
    10	export interface BlockCoreDefinition<TSchema extends ZodTypeAny = ZodTypeAny> {
    11	  /** kebab-case identifier，全局唯一 */
    12	  readonly name: string;
    13	  readonly kind: BlockKind;
    14	  readonly propsSchema: TSchema;
    15	  /** MDX 中实际使用的 PascalCase 组件名，例如 "Callout" / "JupyterBlock" */
    16	  readonly mdxComponent: string;
    17	}
    18	
    19	export interface BlockViewProps<TSchema extends ZodTypeAny> {
    20	  readonly props: ZodInfer<TSchema>;
    21	  readonly content?: string;
    22	  /** Wave 2 起追加 runtime context（kernel session / theme / etc.） */
    23	}
    24	
    25	/**
    26	 * BlockUI：presentational 层 —— React 组件，绑定到已注册的 core。
    27	 * 同一 core 可挂多个 UI（uiId 区分），Adopter 可写自己的 UI 替代 ui-default。
    28	 */
    29	export interface BlockUIDefinition<TSchema extends ZodTypeAny = ZodTypeAny> {
    30	  /** 必须指向已 registerCore 的 core */
    31	  readonly coreName: string;
    32	  /** UI 实现 id：'default' / 'minimal' / 用户自定义 */
    33	  readonly uiId: string;
    34	  readonly EditorView: ComponentType<BlockViewProps<TSchema>>;
    35	  readonly RenderView: ComponentType<BlockViewProps<TSchema>>;
    36	}
    37	
    38	export function defineCore<T extends ZodTypeAny>(
    39	  def: BlockCoreDefinition<T>,
    40	): BlockCoreDefinition<T> {
    41	  return def;
    42	}
    43	
    44	export function defineUI<T extends ZodTypeAny>(
    45	  def: BlockUIDefinition<T>,
    46	): BlockUIDefinition<T> {
    47	  return def;
    48	}
    49	
    50	/**
    51	 * BlockRegistry：双层注册表（ADR-0003）。
    52	 * registerCore：挂业务定义；registerUI：挂视觉实现。
    53	 * 同 core 多 UI 场景：getUI(name) 不带 uiId 时取首个注册的（约定为 'default'）。
    54	 */
    55	export class BlockRegistry {
    56	  readonly #cores = new Map<string, BlockCoreDefinition>();
    57	  readonly #uis = new Map<string, BlockUIDefinition[]>();
    58	
    59	  registerCore(core: BlockCoreDefinition): void {
    60	    if (this.#cores.has(core.name)) {
    61	      throw new Error(`Duplicate core name: ${core.name}`);
    62	    }
    63	    this.#cores.set(core.name, core);
    64	  }
    65	
    66	  registerUI(ui: BlockUIDefinition): void {
    67	    if (!this.#cores.has(ui.coreName)) {
    68	      throw new Error(`Unknown core for UI registration: ${ui.coreName}`);
    69	    }
    70	    const existing = this.#uis.get(ui.coreName) ?? [];
    71	    if (existing.some((u) => u.uiId === ui.uiId)) {
    72	      throw new Error(
    73	        `Duplicate UI registration: core=${ui.coreName} uiId=${ui.uiId}`,
    74	      );
    75	    }
    76	    existing.push(ui);
    77	    this.#uis.set(ui.coreName, existing);
    78	  }
    79	
    80	  getCore(name: string): BlockCoreDefinition | undefined {
    81	    return this.#cores.get(name);
    82	  }
    83	
    84	  listCores(): readonly BlockCoreDefinition[] {
    85	    return [...this.#cores.values()];
    86	  }
    87	
    88	  /** 不传 uiId 时返回首个（约定为 'default'）；传了精确匹配 */
    89	  getUI(coreName: string, uiId?: string): BlockUIDefinition | undefined {
    90	    const list = this.#uis.get(coreName);
    91	    if (!list || list.length === 0) return undefined;
    92	    if (uiId === undefined) return list[0];
    93	    return list.find((u) => u.uiId === uiId);
    94	  }
    95	
    96	  listUIs(coreName: string): readonly BlockUIDefinition[] {
    97	    return [...(this.#uis.get(coreName) ?? [])];
    98	  }
    99	}

     1	import katex from 'katex';
     2	import type { BlockViewProps } from '@skb/block-foundation';
     3	import { mathCore } from '../core/core-definition';
     4	
     5	/**
     6	 * KaTeX 渲染：用 katex.renderToString 一次产 HTML 字符串注入 React DOM。
     7	 * 与 Math.astro 共享同一渲染路径（KaTeX 自身），保证 editor / SSR / NodeView
     8	 * 三处 visual output byte-equivalent（ADR-0006 item #5 algorithm replication audit）。
     9	 *
    10	 * 错误处理：throwOnError=false 让 KaTeX 把无效 LaTeX 渲染成红色 .katex-error 块；
    11	 * 不静默吞错（spec 要求 "red error box with the source visible"）。
    12	 */
    13	export const renderMath = (expression: string, display: boolean): string =>
    14	  katex.renderToString(expression, {
    15	    displayMode: display,
    16	    throwOnError: false,
    17	    output: 'html',
    18	    strict: 'ignore',
    19	  });
    20	
    21	/**
    22	 * MathView 接受 inferred props ({ expression, display }) — 与
    23	 * `mathCore.propsSchema` 的 ZodInfer 等价。函数式组件而非
    24	 * `ComponentType<BlockViewProps<typeof ...>>` 显式标注，避免 generic 变 invariant
    25	 * 在 BlockRegistry.registerUI 处与 ZodTypeAny widening 冲突（exactOptionalPropertyTypes 触发）。
    26	 */
    27	export function MathView({
    28	  props,
    29	}: BlockViewProps<typeof mathCore.propsSchema>): JSX.Element {
    30	  const html = renderMath(props.expression, props.display);
    31	  const Tag = props.display ? 'div' : 'span';
    32	  return (
    33	    <Tag
    34	      data-block="math"
    35	      data-display={props.display ? 'true' : 'false'}
    36	      // KaTeX 输出受信（npm 包内部生成），无 user HTML 注入路径
    37	      dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: html }}
    38	    />
    39	  );
    40	}
    41	
    42	export const MathEditorView = MathView;
    43	export const MathRenderView = MathView;

docs/audits/codex-runs/2026-04-30-task-B2-pr-gate.txt:5817:2. [visual-smoke.spec.ts](/home/weiyi/selfKnowledgeBaseWeb/apps/site/src/__tests__/visual-smoke.spec.ts:15) clicks the theme button immediately after `page.goto('/')`. The button is SSR-rendered through [ThemeToggle.astro](/home/weiyi/selfKnowledgeBaseWeb/apps/site/src/components/ThemeToggle.astro:5), while the React `onClick` is attached only after Astro `client:load` hydration. Playwright waits for actionability, not event listener attachment, so slow CI can swallow the click and time out at line 16. Add an explicit hydration wait before clicking.

[visual-smoke.spec.ts](/home/weiyi/selfKnowledgeBaseWeb/apps/site/src/__tests__/visual-smoke.spec.ts:15) clicks the theme button immediately after `page.goto('/')`. The button is SSR-rendered through [ThemeToggle.astro](/home/weiyi/selfKnowledgeBaseWeb/apps/site/src/components/ThemeToggle.astro:5), while the React `onClick` is attached only after Astro `client:load` hydration. Playwright waits for actionability, not event listener attachment, so slow CI can swallow the click and time out at line 16. Add an explicit hydration wait before clicking.

    78	### D4: BlockRegistry 升级为 Core + UI 双层
    79	
    80	`packages/block-foundation/src/registry.ts` 从单一 `BlockDefinition` 拆成：
    81	
    82	```typescript
    83	export interface BlockCoreDefinition<TSchema extends ZodTypeAny = ZodTypeAny> {
    84	  readonly name: string;                   // unique core id
    85	  readonly kind: 'prose' | 'component';
    86	  readonly propsSchema: TSchema;
    87	  readonly mdxComponent: string;           // PascalCase MDX component name
    88	}
    89	
    90	export interface BlockUIDefinition<TSchema extends ZodTypeAny = ZodTypeAny> {
    91	  readonly coreName: string;               // refers back to a registered core
    92	  readonly uiId: string;                   // 'default' | 'minimal' | 用户自定义
    93	  readonly EditorView: ComponentType<BlockViewProps<TSchema>>;
    94	  readonly RenderView: ComponentType<BlockViewProps<TSchema>>;
    95	}
    96	
    97	export class BlockRegistry {
    98	  registerCore(core: BlockCoreDefinition): void;
    99	  registerUI(ui: BlockUIDefinition): void;       // throws if core unknown
   100	  getCore(name: string): BlockCoreDefinition | undefined;
   101	  listCores(): readonly BlockCoreDefinition[];
   102	  getUI(coreName: string, uiId?: string): BlockUIDefinition | undefined;  // default to first
   103	  listUIs(coreName: string): readonly BlockUIDefinition[];
   104	}
   105	```
   106	
   107	同一 core 可挂多个 UI（`uiId` 区分），`getUI(name)` 不带 `uiId` 时返回第一个注册的（约定为 `'default'`）。

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	# RFC: How a `block-*` package registers with `@skb/block-foundation`
     2	
     3	> **Audience**: `simple-block-eng` / `render-block-eng` / `viz-block-eng` writing
     4	> `block-callout` / `block-code` / `block-image` / `block-math` / `block-pdf` /
     5	> `block-jupyter` / `block-nn-viz` / `block-agent-flow` cores; `ux-ui-lead`
     6	> writing the matching `ui-default/` halves.
     7	>
     8	> **Authority**: [ADR-0008](../../docs/decisions/ADR-0008-wave-2-entry-policies.md)
     9	> D2 freezes the registration interface as of 2026-04-30 (commit `b3581bc`).
    10	> This RFC is the consumer-facing walkthrough; the public API surface itself
    11	> lives in [`CONTRACT.md`](./CONTRACT.md).
    12	
    13	This RFC is **not** a spec — `CONTRACT.md` is. It is a step-by-step recipe
    14	showing how the first real consumer (`block-callout`, Task C1/C2) wires its
    15	core + UI into a `BlockRegistry`. Subsequent `block-*` PRs follow the same
    16	shape; cloned `block-code` / `block-image` cores (Tasks C3-C5b) inherit
    17	this structure verbatim.
    18	
    19	## 1. core/ side
    20	
    21	`core/` is the headless half (ADR-0003). No React, no Tiptap; just a Zod
    22	schema + identifying metadata. Place it at `packages/<block>/src/core/`
    23	and export through the package's `core` subpath.
    24	
    25	```typescript
    26	// packages/block-callout/src/core/core-definition.ts
    27	import { defineCore, type BlockCoreDefinition } from '@skb/block-foundation';
    28	import { z } from 'zod';
    29	
    30	const propsSchema = z
    31	  .object({
    32	    variant: z.enum(['note', 'tip', 'warning', 'danger']),
    33	    title: z.string().optional(),
    34	  })
    35	  .strict();
    36	
    37	export const calloutCore: BlockCoreDefinition<typeof propsSchema> = defineCore({
    38	  name: 'callout',
    39	  kind: 'component',
    40	  propsSchema,
    41	  mdxComponent: 'Callout',
    42	});
    43	```
    44	
    45	Notes that map directly to `BlockCoreDefinition` fields in
    46	[`src/registry.ts`](./src/registry.ts):
    47	
    48	- `name`: kebab-case, globally unique. Used as the registry key — duplicate
    49	  registration throws `Duplicate core name: <name>` at runtime.
    50	- `kind`: `'prose' | 'component'`. Wave 2 blocks are all `'component'`;
    51	  `'prose'` is reserved for behaviors covered by `proseExtensions` and is
    52	  closed to new entries (CONTRACT invariant: "Prose blocks 零自写代码").
    53	- `propsSchema`: must be a `z.object(...).strict()`; every nested
    54	  `ZodObject` must independently call `.strict()`. See
    55	  [`CONTRACT.md` § Invariants — Schema strictness](./CONTRACT.md#invariants)
    56	  + [ADR-0006](../../docs/decisions/ADR-0006-asymmetry-audit-checklist.md) item #3
    57	  (`.strict()` does not propagate into nested objects). The current type
    58	  signature `BlockCoreDefinition<TSchema extends ZodTypeAny>` is wider than
    59	  `ZodObject`; type-narrowing is deferred to Wave 3, so for Wave 2 this is
    60	  a behavior contract enforced by RFC + reviewer + integration tests, not
    61	  by TypeScript. Skipping `.strict()` does not throw — `propsSchema.parse`
    62	  silently strips unknown keys, which is exactly the contract-drift class
    63	  this rule prevents.
    64	- `mdxComponent`: PascalCase, must match the JSX tag used in `.mdx` files
    65	  exactly (the MDX bridge uses this string when serializing back). For
    66	  `block-callout`, MDX content reads `<Callout variant="note">…</Callout>`
    67	  so this field is `'Callout'`.
    68	
    69	`defineCore` is a no-op identity helper that exists purely to give TypeScript
    70	inference a hand — the returned object satisfies `BlockCoreDefinition<T>`
    71	with `T` inferred from `propsSchema`. Equivalent to writing the type
    72	annotation manually. `defineUI` (used in §2) is the symmetric helper for
    73	`BlockUIDefinition<T>`; both helpers exist for inference convenience only,
    74	so consumers can write `defineUI({ ... })` without manually spelling
    75	`<typeof calloutCore.propsSchema>`.
    76	
    77	## 2. ui-default/ side
    78	
    79	`ui-default/` is the presentational half. It imports the core (never inline-
    80	redefines its schema — see ADR-0006 item #4: "single-authority schema → audit
    81	every consumer for inline duplicates"), then attaches React components for
    82	the editor and renderer.
    83	
    84	```typescript
    85	// packages/block-callout/src/ui-default/index.ts
    86	import { defineUI, type BlockUIDefinition } from '@skb/block-foundation';
    87	import { calloutCore } from '../core/core-definition';
    88	import { CalloutEditorView } from './editor-view';
    89	import { CalloutRenderView } from './render-view';
    90	
    91	export const calloutUIDefault: BlockUIDefinition<
    92	  typeof calloutCore.propsSchema
    93	> = defineUI({
    94	  coreName: calloutCore.name, // must reference an already-registered core
    95	  uiId: 'default', // convention: first-registered UI is 'default'
    96	  EditorView: CalloutEditorView,
    97	  RenderView: CalloutRenderView,
    98	});
    99	```
   100	
   101	The two view components share the `BlockViewProps<TSchema>` shape from
   102	[`src/registry.ts`](./src/registry.ts):
   103	
   104	```typescript
   105	// packages/block-callout/src/ui-default/editor-view.tsx
   106	import type { ComponentType } from 'react';
   107	import type { BlockViewProps } from '@skb/block-foundation';
   108	import { calloutCore } from '../core/core-definition';
   109	
   110	export const CalloutEditorView: ComponentType<
   111	  BlockViewProps<typeof calloutCore.propsSchema>
   112	> = ({ props, content }) => {
   113	  // props.variant is z-inferred to 'note' | 'tip' | 'warning' | 'danger'
   114	  // props.title is z-inferred to string | undefined
   115	  // content is the optional Tiptap inner content as a string
   116	  return (
   117	    <aside data-variant={props.variant}>
   118	      {props.title ? <strong>{props.title}</strong> : null}
   119	      <div>{content}</div>
   120	    </aside>
   121	  );
   122	};
   123	```
   124	
   125	`RenderView` follows the same shape but renders for static output (Astro
   126	build / SSR path) rather than the editor. UX/visual conventions for the
   127	component itself (tokens, spacing, accessibility) are owned by `ux-ui-lead`
   128	via `packages/design-tokens/CONTRACT.md`; this RFC fixes only the
   129	registration plumbing.
   130	
   131	### Wiring at boot
   132	
   133	`registerCore` must precede `registerUI` for the same `coreName`; otherwise
   134	`registerUI` throws `Unknown core for UI registration: <coreName>`. The
   135	boot path (Wave 3 `editor-shell` / `apps/site` integration) does:
   136	
   137	```typescript
   138	import { BlockRegistry } from '@skb/block-foundation';
   139	import { calloutCore } from '@skb/block-callout/core';
   140	import { calloutUIDefault } from '@skb/block-callout/ui-default';
   141	
   142	const registry = new BlockRegistry();
   143	registry.registerCore(calloutCore);
   144	registry.registerUI(calloutUIDefault);
   145	
   146	// later, render-time lookup:
   147	const ui = registry.getUI('callout'); // returns calloutUIDefault (uiId='default')
   148	const ui2 = registry.getUI('callout', 'minimal'); // exact match by uiId
   149	```
   150	
   151	When multiple UIs are registered for one core, `getUI(name)` without a
   152	`uiId` returns the **first registered** (CONTRACT invariant — convention:
   153	register `'default'` first). `getUI(name, uiId)` does exact match.
   154	
   155	## 3. Consumer-side test template
   156	
   157	Each `block-*` package ships an integration test that wires its own core +
   158	UI into a fresh registry and verifies the round-trip. This is the minimum
   159	surface; package-specific tests (props validation, schema edge cases, view
   160	rendering) live alongside.
   161	
   162	```typescript
   163	// packages/block-callout/src/__tests__/registry-integration.test.ts
   164	import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
   165	import { BlockRegistry } from '@skb/block-foundation';
   166	import { calloutCore } from '../core/core-definition';
   167	import { calloutUIDefault } from '../ui-default';
   168	
   169	describe('block-callout registry integration', () => {
   170	  it('registers core + UI and resolves both', () => {
   171	    const reg = new BlockRegistry();
   172	    reg.registerCore(calloutCore);
   173	    reg.registerUI(calloutUIDefault);
   174	
   175	    expect(reg.getCore('callout')).toBe(calloutCore);
   176	    expect(reg.getUI('callout')).toBe(calloutUIDefault);
   177	    expect(reg.getUI('callout', 'default')).toBe(calloutUIDefault);
   178	  });
   179	
   180	  it('propsSchema accepts every declared variant', () => {
   181	    for (const variant of ['note', 'tip', 'warning', 'danger'] as const) {
   182	      const parsed = calloutCore.propsSchema.parse({ variant });
   183	      expect(parsed.variant).toBe(variant);
   184	    }
   185	  });
   186	
   187	  it('propsSchema rejects unknown variants and unknown keys', () => {
   188	    expect(() => calloutCore.propsSchema.parse({ variant: 'bogus' })).toThrow();
   189	    expect(() =>
   190	      calloutCore.propsSchema.parse({ variant: 'note', extra: 1 }),
   191	    ).toThrow(); // .strict() — extra key rejected
   192	  });
   193	});
   194	```
   195	
   196	The patterns mirror
   197	[`src/__tests__/registry.test.ts`](./src/__tests__/registry.test.ts) in this
   198	package — the foundation owns registry-mechanics tests; each `block-*` owns
   199	the integration test for its own core+UI pair.
   200	
   201	## 4. Error scenarios
   202	
   203	### 4.1 Runtime errors thrown by `BlockRegistry`
   204	
   205	These come from the methods in [`src/registry.ts`](./src/registry.ts). Workers
   206	should write tests asserting each one fires for their block where applicable.
   207	
   208	| Scenario                                        | Method        | Error message                                              |
   209	| ----------------------------------------------- | ------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------- |
   210	| Register two cores with the same `name`         | `registerCore` | `Duplicate core name: <name>`                              |
   211	| `registerUI` before `registerCore` (same name)  | `registerUI`   | `Unknown core for UI registration: <coreName>`             |
   212	| Two UIs with same `(coreName, uiId)`            | `registerUI`   | `Duplicate UI registration: core=<coreName> uiId=<uiId>`   |
   213	| `getCore(name)` for an unregistered name        | `getCore`      | returns `undefined` (no throw)                             |
   214	| `getUI(coreName)` with no UIs registered        | `getUI`        | returns `undefined` (no throw)                             |
   215	| `getUI(coreName, uiId)` with non-matching uiId  | `getUI`        | returns `undefined` (no throw)                             |
   216	
   217	### 4.2 Compile-time errors (caught by `pnpm typecheck`)
   218	
   219	`BlockCoreDefinition` field errors:
   220	
   221	| Scenario                              | TypeScript error (representative)                                  |
   222	| ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ |
   223	| `name` missing                        | `Property 'name' is missing in type ...`                           |
   224	| `name` not a `string`                 | `Type '<X>' is not assignable to type 'string'`                    |
   225	| `kind` missing                        | `Property 'kind' is missing in type ...`                           |
   226	| `kind` not `'prose' \| 'component'`   | `Type '"<X>"' is not assignable to type 'BlockKind'`               |
   227	| `propsSchema` missing                 | `Property 'propsSchema' is missing in type ...`                    |
   228	| `propsSchema` not a Zod schema        | `Type '<X>' is not assignable to type 'ZodTypeAny'`                |
   229	| `mdxComponent` missing                | `Property 'mdxComponent' is missing in type ...`                   |
   230	| `mdxComponent` not a `string`         | `Type '<X>' is not assignable to type 'string'`                    |
   231	
   232	`BlockUIDefinition` field errors (symmetric to `BlockCoreDefinition`: 4 fields × {missing, wrong-type} = 8 rows + props-shape divergence):
   233	
   234	| Scenario                                                | TypeScript error (representative)                                                            |
   235	| ------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
   236	| `coreName` missing                                      | `Property 'coreName' is missing in type ...`                                                 |
   237	| `coreName` not a `string`                               | `Type '<X>' is not assignable to type 'string'`                                              |
   238	| `uiId` missing                                          | `Property 'uiId' is missing in type ...`                                                     |
   239	| `uiId` not a `string`                                   | `Type '<X>' is not assignable to type 'string'`                                              |
   240	| `EditorView` missing                                    | `Property 'EditorView' is missing in type ...`                                               |
   241	| `EditorView` not a `ComponentType<BlockViewProps<T>>`   | `Type '<X>' is not assignable to type 'ComponentType<BlockViewProps<TSchema>>'`              |
   242	| `RenderView` missing                                    | `Property 'RenderView' is missing in type ...`                                               |
   243	| `RenderView` not a `ComponentType<BlockViewProps<T>>`   | `Type '<X>' is not assignable to type 'ComponentType<BlockViewProps<TSchema>>'`              |
   244	| `props` shape passed to view diverges from `TSchema`    | `Type '<inferred>' does not satisfy ...`（出现在 view 实现内 destructure / use point）       |
   245	
   246	What TypeScript does NOT catch (covered by runtime throws above + integration test + reviewer):
   247	
   248	- `coreName` typo (it's a `string`; runtime `Unknown core for UI registration: ...` catches at boot)
   249	- `name` collision across packages (runtime `Duplicate core name: ...` catches at register time)
   250	- `mdxComponent` PascalCase casing (string-level; reviewer + integration test catches)
   251	- `propsSchema` is a Zod schema but **not** `z.object(...).strict()` (e.g. `z.string()`, `z.array(...)`, or `z.object(...)` without `.strict()`): the type signature only requires `ZodTypeAny`, so TypeScript accepts it; `propsSchema.parse(input)` does not throw but silently strips unknown keys, which is the contract-drift risk [`CONTRACT.md` § Invariants — Schema strictness](./CONTRACT.md#invariants) closes via reviewer + integration test (type-narrow to `ZodObject` deferred to Wave 3)
   252	
   253	### 4.3 Defensive-copy guarantee
   254	
   255	`listCores()` and `listUIs(coreName)` return fresh arrays per call (CONTRACT
   256	[Invariants](./CONTRACT.md#invariants) — "Defensive copy"). Consumers can
   257	freely mutate the returned arrays without leaking into the registry. Don't
   258	write tests that assume identity (`reg.listCores() === reg.listCores()`) —
   259	they're new arrays each time. Implementation: `src/registry.ts:84` + `:96`.
   260

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
     2	import { BlockRegistry, type BlockUIDefinition } from '@skb/block-foundation';
     3	import { mathCore } from '../core/core-definition';
     4	import { mathUiDefault } from '../ui-default/math.ui';
     5	
     6	/**
     7	 * mathUiDefault 类型为 BlockUIDefinition<typeof mathCore.propsSchema>
     8	 * (defineUI 从 EditorView/RenderView 的 BlockViewProps<TSchema> 推 generic)。
     9	 * BlockRegistry.registerUI 形参为 BlockUIDefinition<ZodTypeAny>，因 ComponentType
    10	 * 在 props 上 contravariant + exactOptionalPropertyTypes，narrow → wide 的赋值
    11	 * 不通过；registerUI 运行期仅按 coreName/uiId 查表，不再触碰 EditorView 的具体
    12	 * props 形状。这里 cast 到 wide 类型是 type-system 工件，行为安全。
    13	 *
    14	 * 关键：cast 不是 disable type-checking — 仍校验到 BlockUIDefinition；
    15	 * 仅 widening generic param 一处。Wave 3 类型 narrow 到 ZodObject 时（block-foundation
    16	 * CONTRACT § Schema strictness 提到的 deferred 工作）此 cast 会自然消失。
    17	 */
    18	const wideMathUI = mathUiDefault as unknown as BlockUIDefinition;
    19	
    20	describe('block-math registry integration', () => {
    21	  it('registerCore + getCore round-trip preserves identity', () => {
    22	    const reg = new BlockRegistry();
    23	    reg.registerCore(mathCore);
    24	    expect(reg.getCore('math')).toBe(mathCore);
    25	  });
    26	
    27	  it('registerUI + getUI round-trip preserves identity', () => {
    28	    const reg = new BlockRegistry();
    29	    reg.registerCore(mathCore);
    30	    reg.registerUI(wideMathUI);
    31	    expect(reg.getUI('math')).toBe(mathUiDefault);
    32	    expect(reg.getUI('math', 'default')).toBe(mathUiDefault);
    33	  });
    34	
    35	  it('default UI lookup returns first-registered (mathUiDefault uiId="default")', () => {
    36	    const reg = new BlockRegistry();
    37	    reg.registerCore(mathCore);
    38	    reg.registerUI(wideMathUI);
    39	    expect(mathUiDefault.uiId).toBe('default');
    40	    expect(reg.getUI('math')).toBe(mathUiDefault);
    41	  });
    42	
    43	  it('registerUI before registerCore throws Unknown core', () => {
    44	    const reg = new BlockRegistry();
    45	    expect(() => reg.registerUI(wideMathUI)).toThrow(
    46	      /Unknown core for UI registration: math/,
    47	    );
    48	  });
    49	
    50	  it('getCore returns undefined for unknown name', () => {
    51	    const reg = new BlockRegistry();
    52	    expect(reg.getCore('not-math')).toBeUndefined();
    53	  });
    54	});

/bin/bash -lc "nl -ba packages/design-tokens/CONTRACT.md | sed -n '1,180p'; nl -ba packages/design-tokens/src/tokens.css | sed -n '1,160p'" in /home/weiyi/selfKnowledgeBaseWeb

     1	# @skb/design-tokens Contract
     2	
     3	## Public surface
     4	
     5	- `tokens.css` — `:root` light theme CSS variables (default)
     6	- `tokens-dark.css` — `:root[data-theme="dark"]` overrides
     7	- `tailwind-preset` — Tailwind 3.x preset (color / spacing / typography / radius / shadow / motion); ships with a `.d.cts` so consumers' tsconfig (strict, no `allowJs`) can `import preset from '@skb/design-tokens/tailwind-preset'` without `// @ts-ignore`
     8	- `tokens` / `colorVars` / `spaceVars` — TS mirrors for CSS-in-JS / programmatic access
     9	- `useTheme` — React hook returning `{ theme, setTheme, toggle }`
    10	- `getInitialTheme` — pure derivation of the boot theme (localStorage > `prefers-color-scheme` > `'light'`)
    11	- `applyThemeDOM(theme)` — DOM-only, idempotent. Sets / clears `data-theme="dark"` on `<html>`. **Does NOT touch localStorage.** Safe to call from inline FOUC scripts and on every effect tick.
    12	- `STORAGE_KEY` — `'skb-theme'`; the localStorage key shared with the inline FOUC script
    13	- `ThemeToggle` — default switch button component
    14	- `themeNames` / `ThemeName` / `UseThemeResult` — type-level surface
    15	
    16	## Invariants (ADR-0003)
    17	
    18	- **CSS var names are stable**: consumers (apps/site, every block ui-default) depend on the literal var names; renaming is a contract break and requires an ADR.
    19	- **Light + dark must share the same key set**: only values may differ. Adding a new var REQUIRES adding it to both files at the same time.
    20	- **`STORAGE_KEY` is shared with apps/site BaseLayout**: the inline FOUC `<script>` reads `'skb-theme'` literally; any rename must update both packages in the same PR.
    21	- **Tailwind preset is the single source of truth**: `apps/site` and every `block-*/ui-default/` MUST consume this preset. Hand-rolling `theme.colors` or hard-coding color / spacing / typography values is rejected by `pr-gate`.
    22	- **Persistence is coupled to manual user action only** (ADR-0003 D6): `useTheme`'s mount effect calls `applyThemeDOM` only — it never writes localStorage. Persistence happens in `setTheme` / `toggle`. This preserves the property "system OS theme change is reflected on next visit unless the user has explicitly chosen a theme."
    23	
    24	## Inverse-direction obligation: replicas of pre-hydration algorithms
    25	
    26	Some algorithms in this package must be replicated in inline code that runs **before any module loads** (e.g. an Astro `<script is:inline>` in `<head>` that prevents FOUC), and therefore cannot import from `@skb/design-tokens`. Those replicas carry an inverse-direction obligation: the authority lives here, but the correctness contract binds every consumer that ships its own copy.
    27	
    28	**Currently in scope**: `getInitialTheme()` in `src/use-theme.ts` (the boot-theme derivation: localStorage → `prefers-color-scheme` → `'light'` default).
    29	
    30	Any consumer that replicates this algorithm in inline code MUST be byte-equivalent to the authority in BOTH happy-path AND exception handling:
    31	
    32	1. **Strict-whitelist saved value**: only `saved === 'light' || saved === 'dark'` honors the stored value; anything else falls through to `matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)')`. A truthy-coerce check (e.g. `saved ? saved === 'dark' : ...`) diverges for invalid / case-mismatched / legacy localStorage values and produces a hydration flash on first paint.
    33	2. **Narrow `try/catch` scope**: the `try/catch` MUST wrap ONLY the `localStorage.getItem` call. `matchMedia` + DOM apply MUST run AFTER the catch handler. A wide-scope `try/catch` swallows the `matchMedia` path on Safari Private Mode / iOS WebView with storage restrictions / browsers with localStorage disabled — producing light when system pref is dark, i.e. hydration flash.
    34	3. **Hardcoded storage key literal**: replicas hardcode `'skb-theme'` (the value of `STORAGE_KEY` in `src/use-theme.ts`) because they cannot import the constant at FOUC-script time. Renaming `STORAGE_KEY` is a contract break that requires updating every replica in the same PR.
    35	
    36	**Required regression test**: every consumer with an inline replica MUST register a test that asserts byte-equivalence against the authority. Template: `apps/site/src/__tests__/fouc-script.test.ts` — extracts the IIFE from the consumer file, evaluates it via `new Function('localStorage', 'window', 'document', iife)` against parametrized fakes, compares output against `getInitialTheme()` for a 16-row saved-value × system-preference corpus + 2 storage-throws × system-preference rows + 1 truthy-coerce regression assertion (20 tests total). The two storage-throws rows are critical: the saved-value corpus alone passes under a wide-scope `try/catch`.
    37	
    38	**Failure mode reference**: Track A v1 review caught happy-path divergence (truthy-coerce); v2 fixed (1) but kept wide-scope `try/catch`; v3 fixed (2). Both v1 and v2 would have shipped visible hydration flash without pr-gate's catch.
    39	
    40	**Current consumers**: `apps/site` (Wave 1, `src/layouts/BaseLayout.astro`). Wave 2+ consumers (e.g. additional Astro apps, future open-source `apps/demo`) MUST follow this template.
    41	
    42	## Consumer rules
    43	
    44	- **Hydration**: `useTheme` and `ThemeToggle` MUST be used inside a client-only Astro island (`client:load` minimum). The hook reads `window.matchMedia` and `window.localStorage` at first render — passive SSR markup is fine, but island hydration directive must be `client:load` or stricter.
    45	- **FOUC bridge** (apps/site responsibility): the BaseLayout `<script is:inline>` in `<head>` must read `localStorage.getItem('skb-theme')` literally, fall back to `matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches`, and set `data-theme="dark"` on `<html>` accordingly — **before** any React island hydrates. The inline script must NOT write localStorage. The detailed correctness obligation (algorithm + try/catch scope + literal sync + required regression test) is enumerated in the **Inverse-direction obligation** section above.
    46	
    47	### `block-*/ui-default/` consumer norms (Wave 2, anchored by `block-callout` C2)
    48	
    49	These norms apply to every `packages/block-*/src/ui-default/` consumer. The
    50	first consumer (`@skb/block-callout/ui-default/`) is the canonical template;
    51	codex-block-generator clones must preserve them.
    52	
    53	- **No hardcoded color literals**: every `color`, `background-color`, `border-color`
    54	  in `*.css` MUST resolve via `rgb(var(--color-X))` or `rgb(var(--color-X) / <alpha>)`.
    55	  No `#hex`, no named colors, no `rgb(<numeric-triple>)`. `pr-gate` greps for
    56	  these in `packages/block-*/src/ui-default/*.css` and rejects.
    57	- **No inline `style={{ … }}` for variant colors**: happy-dom (used by `vitest run`)
    58	  rejects `rgb(var(--color-…))` in inline styles and silently drops the attribute,
    59	  which would mask real visual regressions. Variant colors MUST live in `*.css`,
    60	  selected by `[data-<block>-variant="…"]` on a stable container element. Inline
    61	  `style` is allowed only for layout-time computed values (e.g., a known px width).
    62	- **Type-only `@skb/design-tokens` import satisfies ADR-0008 D1**: `block-*/ui-default/`
    63	  packages typically don't need `colorVars` / `tokens` at runtime (CSS does the var
    64	  resolution). To keep `@skb/design-tokens` as a real `package.json#dependencies` entry
    65	  (per ADR-0008 D1), import the `ColorTokenName` type to constrain the variant→token
    66	  mapping (see `block-callout/src/ui-default/variant-tokens.ts`). The lexical
    67	  `from '@skb/design-tokens'` in source is what the structure-auditor monthly grep
    68	  detects; type-only imports under `verbatimModuleSyntax: true` count.
    69	- **Tailwind preset is consumed at app-build, not block-build**: `block-*` packages
    70	  don't compile Tailwind themselves; `apps/site` (and any future Astro/Next consumer)
    71	  loads `@skb/design-tokens/tailwind-preset` and JIT-compiles the class names that
    72	  `block-*/ui-default/` Components emit. Block packages MAY emit Tailwind utility
    73	  classes (e.g., `mt-1`, `flex`) but those classes resolve correctly only when the
    74	  consumer's Tailwind config extends from this preset. For visual rules that must
    75	  work outside Tailwind (e.g., editor-shell standalone preview), put them in the
    76	  block's own `*.css` file with explicit `var(--space-X)` references.
    77	- **Required CSS import path**: each `block-*` package exposes its CSS via
    78	  `package.json#exports['./ui-default/<block>.css']`. App entry MUST import each block's
    79	  CSS once at boot (next to design-tokens' `tokens.css` + `tokens-dark.css`).
    80	- **No emoji as icons**: `VARIANT_ICONS` (or equivalent) MUST be hand-traced SVGs at
    81	  consistent stroke width (1.5–2px) and consistent viewBox (`24 24`). Mixing
    82	  filled/outlined or different stroke widths violates ui-ux-pro-max
    83	  `no-emoji-icons` + `stroke-consistency` + `icon-style-consistent` rules.
    84	- **A11y on the variant container**: emit `role="note"` (or appropriate landmark),
    85	  `aria-label="<Variant> <block>"`, `tabindex="0"` so the block participates in
    86	  keyboard nav. `:focus-visible` styles in `*.css` MUST use `outline` (not
    87	  `box-shadow` — `outline` survives `forced-colors` mode).
    88	- **Light + dark parity**: any color expression MUST resolve correctly under both
    89	  themes via the design-tokens var system. No theme-specific class branches in
    90	  `block-*/ui-default/`; if a token doesn't exist in both themes, propose adding
    91	  it to `tokens.css` + `tokens-dark.css` per "Modifying this file" below.
    92	- **Reduced-motion**: any `transition` / `animation` MUST be wrapped in
    93	  `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference) { … }` (see callout.css Wave 2 pattern).
    94	
    95	## Modifying this file
    96	
    97	- Adding a new var: non-breaking, but must update tokens.css + tokens-dark.css + tokens.ts + tailwind-preset.cjs in the same change.
    98	- Renaming or repurposing an existing var (semantic redefinition): contract break — requires an ADR plus a notification to all consuming packages.
    99	- Adding a new theme beyond light/dark (e.g. sepia, high-contrast): requires an ADR; `STORAGE_KEY` value type expands; downstream FOUC scripts must handle the new value.
   100	- Renaming or removing any exported function/component: contract break — requires an ADR. (Pre-Wave-1 the only such rename was `applyTheme` → `applyThemeDOM` to enforce the persistence-coupled-to-manual-action invariant; package had no external consumers.)
   101	
   102	## Related
   103	
   104	- [ADR-0003 headless / presentational split](../../docs/decisions/ADR-0003-headless-presentational-split.md) — D6 (manual-only persistence)
   105	- [Design spec §1.5 / §2.5 / §2.6](../../docs/superpowers/specs/2026-04-29-self-knowledge-base-design.md)
   106	- `../block-foundation/CONTRACT.md` — block UI registration depends on this preset
   107	- `../../apps/site/CONTRACT.md` — FOUC inline script literal sync constraint
     1	/*
     2	 * @skb/design-tokens — light theme (default).
     3	 *
     4	 * Color values use space-separated rgb integer triples so that the Tailwind
     5	 * preset can compose `rgb(var(--color-xxx) / <alpha-value>)` and support
     6	 * Tailwind opacity modifiers (e.g. `bg-bg/50`). Do NOT switch to #hex.
     7	 *
     8	 * Adding a token here REQUIRES an equivalent entry in tokens-dark.css.
     9	 */

---
name: systematic-debugging
description: Use when encountering any bug, test failure, or unexpected behavior, before proposing fixes

# Systematic Debugging

## Overview

Random fixes waste time and create new bugs. Quick patches mask underlying issues.

**Core principle:** ALWAYS find root cause before attempting fixes. Symptom fixes are failure.

**Violating the letter of this process is violating the spirit of debugging.**

## The Iron Law

```
NO FIXES WITHOUT ROOT CAUSE INVESTIGATION FIRST
```

If you haven't completed Phase 1, you cannot propose fixes.

## When to Use

Use for ANY technical issue:
- Test failures
- Bugs in production
- Unexpected behavior
- Performance problems
- Build failures
- Integration issues

**Use this ESPECIALLY when:**
- Under time pressure (emergencies make guessing tempting)
- "Just one quick fix" seems obvious
- You've already tried multiple fixes
- Previous fix didn't work
- You don't fully understand the issue

**Don't skip when:**
- Issue seems simple (simple bugs have root causes too)
- You're in a hurry (rushing guarantees rework)
- Manager wants it fixed NOW (systematic is faster than thrashing)

## The Four Phases

You MUST complete each phase before proceeding to the next.

### Phase 1: Root Cause Investigation

**BEFORE attempting ANY fix:**

1. **Read Error Messages Carefully**
   - Don't skip past errors or warnings
   - They often contain the exact solution
   - Read stack traces completely
   - Note line numbers, file paths, error codes

2. **Reproduce Consistently**
   - Can you trigger it reliably?
   - What are the exact steps?
   - Does it happen every time?
   - If not reproducible → gather more data, don't guess

3. **Check Recent Changes**
   - What changed that could cause this?
   - Git diff, recent commits
   - New dependencies, config changes
   - Environmental differences

4. **Gather Evidence in Multi-Component Systems**

   **WHEN system has multiple components (CI → build → signing, API → service → database):**

   **BEFORE proposing fixes, add diagnostic instrumentation:**
   ```
   For EACH component boundary:
     - Log what data enters component
     - Log what data exits component
     - Verify environment/config propagation
     - Check state at each layer

   Run once to gather evidence showing WHERE it breaks
   THEN analyze evidence to identify failing component
   THEN investigate that specific component
   ```

   **Example (multi-layer system):**
   ```bash
   # Layer 1: Workflow
   echo "=== Secrets available in workflow: ==="
   echo "IDENTITY: ${IDENTITY:+SET}${IDENTITY:-UNSET}"

   # Layer 2: Build script
   echo "=== Env vars in build script: ==="
   env | grep IDENTITY || echo "IDENTITY not in environment"

   # Layer 3: Signing script
   echo "=== Keychain state: ==="
   security list-keychains
   security find-identity -v

   # Layer 4: Actual signing
   codesign --sign "$IDENTITY" --verbose=4 "$APP"
   ```

   **This reveals:** Which layer fails (secrets → workflow ✓, workflow → build ✗)

5. **Trace Data Flow**

   **WHEN error is deep in call stack:**

   See `root-cause-tracing.md` in this directory for the complete backward tracing technique.

   **Quick version:**
   - Where does bad value originate?
   - What called this with bad value?
   - Keep tracing up until you find the source
   - Fix at source, not at symptom

### Phase 2: Pattern Analysis

**Find the pattern before fixing:**

1. **Find Working Examples**
   - Locate similar working code in same codebase
   - What works that's similar to what's broken?

2. **Compare Against References**
   - If implementing pattern, read reference implementation COMPLETELY
   - Don't skim - read every line
   - Understand the pattern fully before applying

3. **Identify Differences**
   - What's different between working and broken?
   - List every difference, however small
   - Don't assume "that can't matter"

4. **Understand Dependencies**
   - What other components does this need?
   - What settings, config, environment?
   - What assumptions does it make?

### Phase 3: Hypothesis and Testing

**Scientific method:**

1. **Form Single Hypothesis**
   - State clearly: "I think X is the root cause because Y"
   - Write it down
   - Be specific, not vague

2. **Test Minimally**
   - Make the SMALLEST possible change to test hypothesis
   - One variable at a time
   - Don't fix multiple things at once

3. **Verify Before Continuing**
   - Did it work? Yes → Phase 4
   - Didn't work? Form NEW hypothesis
   - DON'T add more fixes on top

4. **When You Don't Know**
   - Say "I don't understand X"
   - Don't pretend to know
   - Ask for help
   - Research more

### Phase 4: Implementation

**Fix the root cause, not the symptom:**

1. **Create Failing Test Case**
   - Simplest possible reproduction
   - Automated test if possible
   - One-off test script if no framework
   - MUST have before fixing
   - Use the `superpowers:test-driven-development` skill for writing proper failing tests

2. **Implement Single Fix**
   - Address the root cause identified
   - ONE change at a time
   - No "while I'm here" improvements
   - No bundled refactoring

3. **Verify Fix**
   - Test passes now?
   - No other tests broken?
   - Issue actually resolved?

4. **If Fix Doesn't Work**
   - STOP
   - Count: How many fixes have you tried?
   - If < 3: Return to Phase 1, re-analyze with new information
   - **If ≥ 3: STOP and question the architecture (step 5 below)**
   - DON'T attempt Fix #4 without architectural discussion

5. **If 3+ Fixes Failed: Question Architecture**

   **Pattern indicating architectural problem:**
   - Each fix reveals new shared state/coupling/problem in different place
   - Fixes require "massive refactoring" to implement
   - Each fix creates new symptoms elsewhere

   **STOP and question fundamentals:**
   - Is this pattern fundamentally sound?
   - Are we "sticking with it through sheer inertia"?
   - Should we refactor architecture vs. continue fixing symptoms?

   **Discuss with your human partner before attempting more fixes**

   This is NOT a failed hypothesis - this is a wrong architecture.

## Red Flags - STOP and Follow Process

If you catch yourself thinking:
- "Quick fix for now, investigate later"
- "Just try changing X and see if it works"
- "Add multiple changes, run tests"
- "Skip the test, I'll manually verify"
- "It's probably X, let me fix that"
- "I don't fully understand but this might work"
- "Pattern says X but I'll adapt it differently"
- "Here are the main problems: [lists fixes without investigation]"
- Proposing solutions before tracing data flow
- **"One more fix attempt" (when already tried 2+)**
- **Each fix reveals new problem in different place**

**ALL of these mean: STOP. Return to Phase 1.**

**If 3+ fixes failed:** Question the architecture (see Phase 4.5)

## your human partner's Signals You're Doing It Wrong

**Watch for these redirections:**
- "Is that not happening?" - You assumed without verifying
- "Will it show us...?" - You should have added evidence gathering
- "Stop guessing" - You're proposing fixes without understanding
- "Ultrathink this" - Question fundamentals, not just symptoms
- "We're stuck?" (frustrated) - Your approach isn't working

**When you see these:** STOP. Return to Phase 1.

## Common Rationalizations

| Excuse | Reality |
|--------|---------|
| "Issue is simple, don't need process" | Simple issues have root causes too. Process is fast for simple bugs. |
| "Emergency, no time for process" | Systematic debugging is FASTER than guess-and-check thrashing. |
| "Just try this first, then investigate" | First fix sets the pattern. Do it right from the start. |
| "I'll write test after confirming fix works" | Untested fixes don't stick. Test first proves it. |
| "Multiple fixes at once saves time" | Can't isolate what worked. Causes new bugs. |
| "Reference too long, I'll adapt the pattern" | Partial understanding guarantees bugs. Read it completely. |
| "I see the problem, let me fix it" | Seeing symptoms ≠ understanding root cause. |
| "One more fix attempt" (after 2+ failures) | 3+ failures = architectural problem. Question pattern, don't fix again. |

## Quick Reference

| Phase | Key Activities | Success Criteria |

---
name: verification-before-completion
description: Use when about to claim work is complete, fixed, or passing, before committing or creating PRs - requires running verification commands and confirming output before making any success claims; evidence before assertions always
---

# Verification Before Completion

## Overview

Claiming work is complete without verification is dishonesty, not efficiency.

**Core principle:** Evidence before claims, always.

**Violating the letter of this rule is violating the spirit of this rule.**

## The Iron Law

```
NO COMPLETION CLAIMS WITHOUT FRESH VERIFICATION EVIDENCE
```

If you haven't run the verification command in this message, you cannot claim it passes.

## The Gate Function

```
BEFORE claiming any status or expressing satisfaction:

1. IDENTIFY: What command proves this claim?
2. RUN: Execute the FULL command (fresh, complete)
3. READ: Full output, check exit code, count failures
4. VERIFY: Does output confirm the claim?
   - If NO: State actual status with evidence
   - If YES: State claim WITH evidence
5. ONLY THEN: Make the claim

Skip any step = lying, not verifying
```

## Common Failures

| Claim | Requires | Not Sufficient |
|-------|----------|----------------|
| Tests pass | Test command output: 0 failures | Previous run, "should pass" |
| Linter clean | Linter output: 0 errors | Partial check, extrapolation |
| Build succeeds | Build command: exit 0 | Linter passing, logs look good |
| Bug fixed | Test original symptom: passes | Code changed, assumed fixed |
| Regression test works | Red-green cycle verified | Test passes once |
| Agent completed | VCS diff shows changes | Agent reports "success" |
| Requirements met | Line-by-line checklist | Tests passing |

## Red Flags - STOP

- Using "should", "probably", "seems to"
- Expressing satisfaction before verification ("Great!", "Perfect!", "Done!", etc.)
- About to commit/push/PR without verification
- Trusting agent success reports
- Relying on partial verification
- Thinking "just this once"
- Tired and wanting work over
- **ANY wording implying success without having run verification**

## Rationalization Prevention

| Excuse | Reality |
|--------|---------|
| "Should work now" | RUN the verification |
| "I'm confident" | Confidence ≠ evidence |
| "Just this once" | No exceptions |
| "Linter passed" | Linter ≠ compiler |
| "Agent said success" | Verify independently |
| "I'm tired" | Exhaustion ≠ excuse |
| "Partial check is enough" | Partial proves nothing |
| "Different words so rule doesn't apply" | Spirit over letter |

## Key Patterns

**Tests:**
```
✅ [Run test command] [See: 34/34 pass] "All tests pass"
❌ "Should pass now" / "Looks correct"
```

**Regression tests (TDD Red-Green):**
```
✅ Write → Run (pass) → Revert fix → Run (MUST FAIL) → Restore → Run (pass)
❌ "I've written a regression test" (without red-green verification)
```

**Build:**
```
✅ [Run build] [See: exit 0] "Build passes"
❌ "Linter passed" (linter doesn't check compilation)
```

**Requirements:**
```
✅ Re-read plan → Create checklist → Verify each → Report gaps or completion
❌ "Tests pass, phase complete"
```

**Agent delegation:**
```
✅ Agent reports success → Check VCS diff → Verify changes → Report actual state
❌ Trust agent report
```

## Why This Matters

From 24 failure memories:
- your human partner said "I don't believe you" - trust broken
- Undefined functions shipped - would crash
- Missing requirements shipped - incomplete features
- Time wasted on false completion → redirect → rework
- Violates: "Honesty is a core value. If you lie, you'll be replaced."

## When To Apply

**ALWAYS before:**
- ANY variation of success/completion claims
- ANY expression of satisfaction
- ANY positive statement about work state
- Committing, PR creation, task completion
- Moving to next task
- Delegating to agents

**Rule applies to:**
- Exact phrases
- Paraphrases and synonyms
- Implications of success
- ANY communication suggesting completion/correctness

## The Bottom Line

**No shortcuts for verification.**

Run the command. Read the output. THEN claim the result.

This is non-negotiable.

Findings:

1. Missing exported subpaths for documented consumer imports.  
   [package.json](/home/weiyi/selfKnowledgeBaseWeb/packages/block-math/package.json:6) only exports `.`, `./core`, `./ui-default`, but [CONTRACT.md](/home/weiyi/selfKnowledgeBaseWeb/packages/block-math/CONTRACT.md:76) says apps/site imports `@skb/block-math/ui-default/Math.astro`, and design-tokens requires each block CSS under `./ui-default/<block>.css`.  
   Fix: add exports for `./ui-default/Math.astro` and `./ui-default/math.css`, then update [CONTRACT.md](/home/weiyi/selfKnowledgeBaseWeb/packages/block-math/CONTRACT.md:8) entry count/surface.

2. `renderMath` is not actually the single rendering authority.  
   [CONTRACT.md](/home/weiyi/selfKnowledgeBaseWeb/packages/block-math/CONTRACT.md:49) says `Math.tsx` and `Math.astro` share `renderMath`, but [Math.tsx](/home/weiyi/selfKnowledgeBaseWeb/packages/block-math/src/ui-default/Math.tsx:13) and [Math.astro](/home/weiyi/selfKnowledgeBaseWeb/packages/block-math/src/ui-default/Math.astro:11) duplicate KaTeX options independently.  
   Fix: move `renderMath` to a non-React `render-math.ts`, import it from both files, and export it from `ui-default/index.ts`.

3. Documented MDX boolean shorthand will parse incorrectly.  
   [CONTRACT.md](/home/weiyi/selfKnowledgeBaseWeb/packages/block-math/CONTRACT.md:43) documents `<Math ... display>`, but [parse.ts](/home/weiyi/selfKnowledgeBaseWeb/packages/block-math/src/core/parse.ts:20) only treats string `"true"` as true. Real MDX boolean attributes are commonly represented as null-valued attributes, so `display` shorthand can become `false`.  
   Fix: widen the attribute value type to include `null`, parse `display={null}` as true, parse `"true"`/`"false"` explicitly, and reject other display strings.

4. D1-required UI/SSR coverage is missing.  
   The plan calls for `core`, `ui-default`, and `ssr-render` tests plus math fixtures at [plan](/home/weiyi/selfKnowledgeBaseWeb/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-04-30-phase-1-wave-2-implementation.md:1041) and [plan](/home/weiyi/selfKnowledgeBaseWeb/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-04-30-phase-1-wave-2-implementation.md:1045). Current package has only core and registry tests.  
   Fix: add tests for `renderMath`, React/Astro render parity, exported CSS/Astro importability, and boolean shorthand parse.

- Headless purity: `src/core` imports only `@skb/block-foundation`, `zod`, and local core files.

- Root tsconfig reference exists at [tsconfig.json](/home/weiyi/selfKnowledgeBaseWeb/tsconfig.json:13).
